API and query timing report

1936 API routes, 86 modules. Measured: every GET route that could be called against a seeded organisation (100,000 contacts, 1,000,000 messages) on one machine, one user at a time, 7 timed calls per route after one warm-up; p95 is the nearest-rank value of those calls.

464routes timed
3critical: p95 over 300 ms
2watch: 200 to 300 ms
459OK: under 200 ms
18statements over 50 ms mean
1472routes without a latency figure
